Findings settled by probes: in-memory javac (default compact diagnostics) reports `a.add(1)` on `List<String>`/`ArrayList<String>` as `compiler.err.prob.found.req`, while `javac -XDrawDiagnostics` reports `compiler.err.cant.apply.symbols`, so the tests assert what `InMemoryCompiler` sees; `Object o = skus.get(1)` on a polluted list does not throw (javac 25 inserts no cast for an `Object` target), stated as a lab observation; `String.valueOf(settings.get(key))` infers `T = char[]` and throws with `[C` in the message.
